## §0. The document's own rules — the claim ledger, the id families, and the `DECLARED-DEFAULT` table

### §0.1 The document's own form rules

1. **Two regions.** The index region above (status line with its single `derivation:` clause, unit/prefix line, scope boundary, part map, closure state) is **required content and carries no pinnable claim of the six classes**; the claim-bearing region is `§0.2` onward, and **every clause there carries its authority and its class inline** (`[<authority> | <class>]`) (`[A1 — the two-region rule is stated here as this document's own declaration; read with `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.1 item 1 — the same rule at a sibling directory's own home | requirement]`]`).
2. **The authority spellings this document uses, each inside the CLOSED five-form enum.**
   - **`A1`** — a **user/architect statement recorded in this workspace**, cited by the tracker or filing row that records it, plus its date;
   - **`A2`** — an **ACTIVE `docs/decisions.md` row**, cited `docs/decisions.md · <id>`;
   - **`A3`** — a **filed spec `§`**, cited `docs/specs/<unit>/contract.md §<n>` or `docs/specs/<unit>/<part>.md §<n>`, **always with its path where the target is outside this file**, because a bare `§n` across two documents is not an edge;
   - **`A4`** — a **dossier row**, cited `docs/specs/<unit>-adoption-dossier.md · <identifier>`;
   - **the inferred form** — the **status** form, which **stands nowhere in this document: `0` `INFERRED` annotations are written here**, because every clause below is carried by a resolving `A1`–`A4` citation.
   **AND ONE DISCLOSURE THIS ITEM OWES, STATED SO IT IS NOT A SILENT BREACH OF THE FORM:** **this document's `§8` carries rows whose SUBJECT is a missing clause in another unit's contract, and each such row is tagged `[A1 | …]` as this document's own declaration rather than cited as an `A3` target — because the artifact the row is ABOUT is the store's or the JS unit's contract and the row does not claim that either says anything it does not say.**
3. **The claim classes are the CLOSED six:** **`requirement`** · **`declared parameter semantics`** · **`state or fail-state declaration`** · **`numeric or census claim`** · **`cross-reference`** · **`ordering claim`**. **Every pinnable claim below carries an authority and one of these six.** **A `DECLARED-DEFAULT` is not a seventh class: it is a claim of the class its own text carries — usually `declared parameter semantics` or `state or fail-state declaration` — whose `alt` cell is mandatory and whose reversal point is an instrument.**
4. **The adjudications this document obeys, stated so they are not mistaken for the corpus's own.** **No clause of this contract is derived from `../Provident-Electron/**`**: that corpus is **context, never a source**, and a disagreement between a clause below and that corpus is therefore intentional and is not evidence of a defect (`A9`).
5. **The typed register's row form and cap.** The register is `§5.1`–`§5.8`, its rows are `P-PUB-n`, **never `F`-rows and never a `§6`/`FS-n` form**, and its cap is **`≤ 8` rows per unit** — **and the cap is PER UNIT, so this unit owes its OWN register and authors no row in any other unit's**

**⟶ AND THE CAP THIS CLAUSE PRINTS IS READ FORWARD HERE, DATED `2026-10-12` BY THE BOUNDED CAP-RE-CUT PASS (`role_spec_writer`), BECAUSE THE CAP IT PHRASES AS A RULE IS THIS UNIT'S AND THIS UNIT'S HAS MOVED.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **this item's own bytes stand BYTE-FOR-BYTE and are read THROUGH this clause; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION, it edits no byte of the item, mints no id and re-decides no rule.** **THE TWO HALVES OF THE ITEM, READ SEPARATELY BECAUSE ONE HAS MOVED AND ONE HAS NOT.** **ONE: *"its cap is `≤ 8` rows per unit"* and *"the cap is PER UNIT, so this unit owes its OWN register and authors no row in any other unit's"* — the PER-UNIT RULE IS UNMOVED and its FIGURE has moved for this unit: THE OPERATIVE CAP IS `≤ 10`, HOLDING AT `10 ≤ 10` ✓, and the unit's operative range is `P-PUB-1`…`P-PUB-10`** (`§5.0`'s extension block; `§5.0a`; `§5.0b`). **TWO: *"the JS unit's register stays `8 ≤ 8` with NO NINTH ROW"* — UNMOVED, TRUE, AND CHECKED RATHER THAN ASSUMED: the cap is PER UNIT, the amendment of `2026-10-12` reaches THIS unit's register alone, and the sibling register stands at its `8` rows with its own cap holding** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.1 item 5 — the per-unit cap and the row form | requirement]`; `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §5.0 — the sibling register's own census | numeric or census claim]`). **SO NO PART OF THE ITEM'S OWN SUBJECT IS FALSIFIED: what a reader must not do is read the item's `≤ 8` as THIS unit's operative cap, which is the reading this clause supplies.** **AND NO BYTE UNDER `docs/specs/storeroom-js/**` IS WRITTEN BY THIS CLAUSE: the sibling surface is cited and never re-spelled.** `[A2 docs/decisions.md · the ACTIVE `A91` row of 2026-10-12; A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §5.0` · `§5.0a` · `§5.0b`; read with `§0.2`'s own families table's `the typed register` row below | numeric or census claim]`; **AND THE REMAINDER OF THAT ITEM'S OWN TEXT, DISPLACED FROM BESIDE ITS FIRST WORDS BY THIS PASS'S INSERTION AND STANDING VERBATIM AS THE ITEM'S CONTINUATION — it reads, at its own bytes: the JS unit's register stays `8 ≤ 8` with NO NINTH ROW** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.1 item 5 and §5.0 — the sibling register's own census, and the no-second-register rule | requirement]`).
6. **The citation-admissibility rule.** **No citation of this document points at a path or an id that does not exist in this repository.** **Where a rule this document obeys comes from a process-rule spec that is NOT filed under this repository, the clause STATES THE RULE ITSELF and tags it as its own declaration, rather than citing a path that cannot be opened** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.1 item 7 — the same rule at a sibling directory's own home | requirement]`).
7. **THE CLOSED-STATUS-CODE RULE, RESTATED WITH ITS RESOLVING CITATION:** **the store's failure channel is a CLOSED SET OF STATUS CODES with GENERATED messages**, `15` members ranked `1`…`15`, whose membership is the STORE contract's to close — **and this unit MINTS NO CODE, WIDENS NO SET, and renames no member** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-native/contract.md §8.1 · §8.2 · §8.3 | cross-reference]`).
8. **THE OMISSION RULE AND THIS UNIT'S OWN OMISSION TABLE.** **A part name that would carry nothing is OMITTED RATHER THAN WRITTEN EMPTY**; **an omitted part is ABSENT, and an empty file at its path is a gate-2 finding**; **every omission is recorded with a one-line reason in the index region's omissions table**; and **the omissions contribute to NEITHER side of the closure predicate** (`[A1 — stated as this document's own rule, with the sibling home cited for its shape: `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.1 item 10 | requirement]`]`).

**⟶ AND ONE RULE THAT STOOD AT THIS DOCUMENT'S FOOT WITH NO `§`-NUMBER IS GIVEN ITS OWN NUMBER HERE, DATED `2026-10-13`, BY THE GATE-2 EIGHT-FINDING CLOSE-OUT PASS (`role_spec_writer`), BECAUSE A RULE A REVIEWER CANNOT ADDRESS BY `§` IS A RULE THAT IS CITABLE BY PROSE ALONE.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **items `1`–`8` above stand BYTE-FOR-BYTE and are read THROUGH this clause; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION at this span's foot, it renumbers NO pre-pass item, edits no byte of any item, mints no id and re-decides no rule; and NO pre-pass CITATION of *"`§0.1` item `5`"* moves, because item `5` keeps its number.**

9. **THE CLOSE-OUT CONVENTION — THE STATUS FORM THIS DOCUMENT'S DATED CLAUSES OBEY, AND THE ADDRESS OF THE RULE ITSELF.** **A CAP OR CENSUS CELL THAT A LATER PASS MOVES IS NEVER RE-SPELLED, EDITED OR STRUCK: THE PRE-PASS STATEMENT STANDS VERBATIM AT ITS OWN LINE, AND A DATED RECONCILIATION CLAUSE STANDS BESIDE IT, NAMING THE OPERATIVE FIGURE, THE ARITHMETIC THAT PRODUCED IT AND THE CLAUSE THAT RULES IT.** **BOTH FIGURES REMAIN ON THE PAGE ON PURPOSE, AND WHERE TWO FIGURES STAND FOR ONE CELL, THE DATED CLAUSE BESIDE THE CELL GOVERNS.** **AND THE RULE IS RECIPROCAL: WHEN A PASS MOVES A CAP, A CLAUSE THAT PRINTS SUCH A FIGURE WITH NO DATE BESIDE IT IS A FINDING AT THE NEXT REVIEW, AND ITS DISPOSITION IS A DATED CLAUSE BESIDE THE CELL AND NEVER A RE-SPELLING OF IT.** **THE RULE'S OWN STATEMENT, WITH ITS EXEMPLARS AND ITS RECIPROCAL HALF, STANDS WHERE IT WAS WRITTEN — AT `§10`'s foot, below the close-out proofreading clause's item `7` — AND ITS ADDRESS IS HERE: IT IS READ AS `contract.md §10`'s foot clause, dated `2026-10-12`, whose prose citation of *"this document's own foot"* is REPLACED BY THAT ADDRESS BY THE CLAUSE THAT FOLLOWS IT.** `[A1 — the rule is stated as this document's own declaration; its site list's corrections and their instrument are declared at `§10`'s foot, in the clause dated `2026-10-13` | requirement]`

### §0.3 THE `DECLARED-DEFAULT` TABLE — every residual, reversible, with its alternative priced and its precondition written

**WHAT THIS TABLE IS.** **Every residual this unit's rulings, its gate-1 record and its filing leave unnamed enters this contract as a DECLARED, REVERSIBLE default** — never as a blocker and never as a question. **Each row states: what it pins · the clause it cites · what it REFUSES · the property that checks it · its priced alternative · its reversal point · and its PRECONDITION where the default's own adoption depends on a fact the release act supplies.** **A `PUB-DD` row is a claim whose class is the one its own text carries, and its `alt` cell is mandatory.** `[A1 | declared parameter semantics]`

**AND THE REVERSAL INSTRUMENT, STATED ONCE AND READ BY EVERY ROW WHOSE SOURCE IS A RULING'S SILENCE OR THIS CONTRACT'S OWN CHOICE:** **a fresh ruling of the architect recorded as an ACTIVE `docs/decisions.md` row, followed by a bounded amendment of `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/` owned by `role_spec_writer` with the amendment's row recorded in this unit's own amendment list**; **where the reversal also moves a JS-unit cell, the bounded amendment of `docs/specs/storeroom-js/` is named beside it**; **no row may be reversed by a later pass's preference, and a park is never a reversal point** (`[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §11 — the seven defaults carry their own reversal points, and the filing's own consequence (1) rules that no default is a silent choice | ordering claim]`).

**AND ONE ROW'S CITATION IS CORRECTED, DATED, SO NO ROW POINTS AT A SECTION THAT DOES NOT CARRY ITS SUBJECT** (`RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **the row's own pins, refusals, property, `alt` price and reversal cell stand byte-for-byte; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION and it records ONE citation cell's correction**): **`PUB-DD-4`'s clause cell is read as `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/implementation-plan.md §2` (`Q11`), because the `Q11` row lives in that file's `§2` PHASE TABLE — the `§3` half of the same cell names THIS document's own release clause and is lawful as it stands.** **The row's citation therefore names a section that CARRIES its subject in each home: the plan-side half names the phase-table row that owns the ledger duty, and the document-side half names this document's `§3`.** **NO ROW IS ADDED, DROPPED OR RENUMBERED BY THIS CORRECTION, AND THE TABLE'S ROW COUNT STAYS `24 = 24` ✓.** `[A1 | cross-reference]`

## §1. The resolution rule and its order — this document's own statement, carried whole by the `resolution` part

**AUTHORITY NOTE FOR THIS SECTION, AND WHAT "CARRIED WHOLE" MEANS HERE.** **This section's clauses are AUTHORED HERE and carried, clause for clause, at `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/resolution.md`** — **the `carrier` part this directory's map declares for it.** **UNLIKE A RELOCATION, NOTHING IS MOVED: this unit's contract document is authored in the same pass as its parts, so the part carries this section's clauses as ITS OWN authored text and a citation to either home names its path plus the `§`-number, because a bare `§n` across two documents is not an edge.** **NO CLAIM GAINS OR LOSES AN AUTHORITY BY STANDING IN BOTH HOMES: the two homes' texts are the SAME clauses, and where this section prints a clause the part repeats it VERBATIM with its authority tag.** `[A1 | cross-reference]`

### §1.1 The three rungs, in order, and nothing else

1. **RUNG ONE — THE SCOPED PLATFORM PACKAGE FOR THIS RUNTIME'S KEY.** **The entry `require`s THE ONE NAME THE CLOSED KEY SET DECLARES FOR THE RUNTIME'S `process.platform` × `process.arch` PAIR, and NO OTHER NAME** (`§1.2`, `§1.3`).
2. **RUNG TWO — THE MODULE-RELATIVE ADDON.** **Failing rung one, the entry `require`s the module-relative path `build/Release/storeroom_js_binding.node` beside the entry module, constructed from the entry module's own location and from no other source** (`PUB-DD-20`; `PAR-PUB-30`).
3. **RUNG THREE — THE DECLARED ABSENT-ADDON FORM.** **Failing rung two, the entry answers the DECLARED ABSENT-ADDON FORM — the store's own `FilePathUnreachable` member carried VERBATIM, with the store's generator's string for that code at the SUBJECT OF THE TWO PATHS THAT WERE TRIED — and NO instance is returned** (`§1.5`; `P-PUB-1`; `P-PUB-5`).

**AND THE ORDER IS THE LAW, STATED SO A DRIVE CAN OBSERVE IT FROM OUTSIDE: NO RUNG IS SKIPPED, REORDERED OR SHORT-CIRCUITED; a rung-two success is NEVER reported as rung one; and rung three is REACHED ONLY WHEN BOTH rung one and rung two have been tried and neither yielded a loadable artefact.** `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §0.3 · `PUB-DD-*`; read with `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §3.3 — the publish story's own matrix, cited and never amended | requirement]`]` — **the class is `ordering claim`.** `[A1 | ordering claim]`

**AND THE ONE CONSEQUENCE OF THE ORDER THAT MUST NOT BE READ AS AN EXCEPTION, STATED PLAINLY: RUNG ONE'S OWN FAILURE IS A MISS, NOT A DECLARED FAILURE, AND IT IS SO BY `A83`'s OWN TERMS** — *"the platform package for this runtime FIRST, the module-relative `build/Release/…node` SECOND (preserving the in-place developer loop), and the DECLARED ABSENT-ADDON FORM THIRD — exactly ONE failure arm"* (`docs/decisions.md · the `A82`/`A83` row of 2026-10-11). **SO A `require` OF AN ABSENT PLATFORM PACKAGE ANSWERS `MODULE_NOT_FOUND` AND THE LOADER MOVES TO RUNG TWO — never a thrown package failure, and never an absorbed state.** `[A2 docs/decisions.md · the `A82`/`A83` row of 2026-10-11 — the order's own words | ordering claim]`

**AND THE CATCH'S DECLARED WIDTH, WITH ITS `alt` PRICED BECAUSE A PINNED SEMANTIC OWES ONE:** **EVERY THROW OF RUNG ONE'S `require` IS TREATED AS A MISS.** **The alternative — CLASSIFYING the throw and moving on only for `MODULE_NOT_FOUND`** — is priced: it would make a PRESENT-BUT-BROKEN platform artefact (`ERR_DLOPEN_FAILED`) answer rung one's own failure shape, which is a SECOND failure shape the single arm does not admit, and it would leave the taxonomy's *present-unloadable* state with no declared answer at a covered key where rung two is available; **available with a fresh ruling, and its reversal is `PUB-DD-*`'s instrument.** `[A1 | declared parameter semantics]`

### §1.2 The total platform table — every pair answered, and no pair left unstated

**THE RUNTIME KEY IS THE PAIR `process.platform` × `process.arch`, AND THE SELECTION IS A TOTAL FUNCTION OF THAT PAIR.** **The table below enumerates the keys; the DECLARED SET of five covered keys, the DECLARED ANSWER for every uncovered pair, and the musl state are all in `resolution.md §4`, whose table is the operative one.** **AND ONE CLAUSE GOVERNS THE TABLE'S CLOSEDNESS, PRINTED HERE SO IT CANNOT BE MISREAD AS A DEFAULT: `libc` IS NOT READ BY ANY CLAUSE OF THIS UNIT — no detection mechanism, no value set and no measured domain is declared for it — so the table's keys are the PAIR alone, and the musl state is a STATE THE LOAD PATH REACHES and not a sixth key** (`PUB-DD-8`; `[A4 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-adoption-dossier.md · the zero-row rationale, `§2.2` item 2 — the watch item this clause obeys | requirement]`).

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `linux` · `x64` | `linux-x64` | `@provident-storeroom/js-linux-x64-gnu` | `-gnu` | **`@auspicion/provident-storeroom-js-linux-x64-gnu`** |
| `linux` · `arm64` | `linux-arm64` | `@provident-storeroom/js-linux-arm64-gnu` | `-gnu` | **`@auspicion/provident-storeroom-js-linux-arm64-gnu`** |
| `darwin` · `x64` | `darwin-x64` | `@provident-storeroom/js-darwin-x64` | none | **`@auspicion/provident-storeroom-js-darwin-x64`** |
| `darwin` · `arm64` | `darwin-arm64` | `@provident-storeroom/js-darwin-arm64` | none | **`@auspicion/provident-storeroom-js-darwin-arm64`** |
| `win32` · `x64` | `win32-x64` | `@provident-storeroom/js-win32-x64-msvc` | `-msvc` | **`@auspicion/provident-storeroom-js-win32-x64-msvc`** |

### §1.4 The state taxonomy — four states, three key classes, and ONE arm

**THE LOAD PATH REACHES EXACTLY FOUR STATES, AND EACH HAS EXACTLY ONE DECLARED ANSWER.** **The states are declared over the RESOLVED ARTEFACT, and the key classes over the runtime:** **the four states are `absent` (no artefact was found at either rung), `present-unloadable` (an artefact was found and its `require` threw), `present-wrong-version` (an artefact loaded at rung one and its own `version` did not match the package's), and `present-right-version` (an artefact loaded and, where the version read applies, its `version` matched).** **The three classes are the `4 × 3` table's columns.** (`P-PUB-5`)

| The key class | `absent` | `present-unloadable` | `present-wrong-version` | `present-right-version` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **COVERED (`§1.2`'s five pairs)** | rung one misses (`MODULE_NOT_FOUND`), rung two misses → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM** (`FilePathUnreachable`, subject = both paths) | rung one throws (`ERR_DLOPEN_FAILED` and every other throw count as a miss), rung two misses → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM**; **where rung two's own artefact is found and throws, the declared absent form stands and its subject is still both paths** | rung one loads, its `version` differs → **THE SINGLE FAILURE ARM** (`FilePathUnreachable`, subject = the rung-one artefact's own path); **no fallback to rung two** | rung one loads and its `version` matches → **SUCCESS**, and no rung-two attempt is made |
| **UNCOVERED (`resolution.md §4`'s declared pairs)** | **RUNG ONE IS NOT ATTEMPTED** (no name exists for the pair, by `C-1`), rung two misses → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM** | **rung one is not attempted**, rung two's found artefact throws → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM** | **UNREACHABLE: the version read applies to rung one ALONE (`PUB-DD-18`), so this cell has no state and a drive that asserts one asserts a state the tree cannot reach** | rung two loads → **SUCCESS**, with NO version comparison performed |
| **MUSL (`linux` · `x64` or `linux` · `arm64` on a musl runtime — a STATE, never a key)** | rung one misses, rung two misses → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM** | **the install-time `-gnu` artefact is present and its `dlopen` fails** → rung one's throw is a miss, rung two misses → **THE DECLARED ABSENT FORM** | **the `-gnu` artefact loads under the runtime's own loader and its `version` differs** → **THE SINGLE FAILURE ARM**; **a `-gnu` artefact whose version MATCHES while the runtime's C library is musl is the DECLARED CONSEQUENCE of `libc`'s absence from `os`/`cpu`, and it lands on the same state its `dlopen` outcome produces** | rung two loads → **SUCCESS**; **where a `-gnu` artefact loads at rung one under a musl runtime its version read applies as at any covered key — the declared answer is the read's, not a libc test's** |

**AND THE `4 × 3` ARITHMETIC, PRINTED WITH ITS TERMS: `4` states × `3` classes = `12` cells, of which `11` carry a declared answer and `1` — the uncovered `present-wrong-version` cell — is DECLARED UNREACHABLE with its reason stated, so NO cell is unstated and NO state the load can reach has two answers.** `[A1 | numeric or census claim]`

1. **THE SINGLE DECLARED FAILURE ARM, ITS FORM AND ITS SUBJECT, FIXED ONCE — AND IT IS THE ONLY FAILURE SHAPE THIS UNIT DECLARES.** **The arm is a THROWN PLAIN OBJECT whose member set is EXACTLY `{status, message}`, its `status` is the store's own `FilePathUnreachable` member carried VERBATIM as a JS `string`, and its `message` is the store's own GENERATOR'S STRING for that code at the SUBJECT THE CLAUSE FIXES.** **NO verb, no construction and no load-path state of this unit answers ANY other failure shape: not a returned union, not an `Error` subclass, not a `Promise` rejection, not a third member, and not a package-authored status.** (`P-PUB-4`; `P-PUB-5`)
2. **THE SUBJECT, IN ONE CLAUSE, AND IT IS ONE CLAUSE FOR EVERY STATE ABOVE:** **the subject is the ADDON ARTEFACT'S OWN PATH — the file the load path reached — and where BOTH rungs were tried and neither yielded a loadable artefact, the declared absent form's subject is BOTH PATHS, rung one's resolved name and rung two's module-relative path, so a consumer on an uncovered key reads exactly which two things were tried.** **`cfg.filePath` is NEVER the subject of this unit's arm** — that path is the STORE'S construction argument, and a `cfg.filePath` that fails the store's own construction-time reachability probe is the SEPARATE state the store's contract declares, reached at the store's own construction and never at the load path (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-native/contract.md §8.1` #13 and `§8.2`'s `FilePathUnreachable` row — the member and its generated string at its own home | state or fail-state declaration]`).
3. **NO FALLBACK EXISTS, AND THIS IS THE WHOLE OF THE CLAUSE.** **The package NEVER falls back to a JavaScript store, NEVER absorbs a state silently, NEVER degrades to a partial instance or a no-op verb, and NEVER substitutes a package-authored message, status or instance for any of the four states.** **AND NO SIXTH OUTCOME EXISTS: the load path is `success`, `the declared absent form`, or `the single failure arm` — three outcomes and no fourth.** (`P-PUB-5`)
4. **THE STATE, ITS STATUS, ITS MESSAGE'S HOME AND THE NO-FALLBACK RULE ARE ALSO DECLARED AT THE PACKAGE'S OWN LOAD STEP, AND THAT DECLARATION IS CITED AND NOT RE-OPENED** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §2.5 item 5 — the absent-or-failed-addon state, its status, its message's home and the no-fallback rule, at the sibling directory's own home | cross-reference]`). **THIS UNIT'S ADDITION IS THE ONE ITS RULING OWNS: THE VERSION READ (`§1.5`), WHICH EXTENDS THE SAME ARM TO A STATE THE SIBLING DECLARATION DOES NOT NAME.** `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §9 · `A84`(b) and `§11` · `A85`(7) | state or fail-state declaration]`
5. **AND THE `alt` THIS CLAUSE CARRIES, PRICED BECAUSE A PINNED ANSWER OWES ONE: A SECOND ANSWER FOR THE UNCOVERED CLASS — for instance a distinct absent form naming the uncovered key as such** — **priced: it gives the failure channel a SECOND producer of a status or a second shape, which `P-PUB-4` and the store's closed set both forbid, and it would make the uncovered consumer's observable differ from a covered consumer's when nothing about their SITUATION differs — both saw two paths tried and no loadable artefact; available with a fresh ruling.** `[A1 | declared parameter semantics]`
6. **WHO AUTHORS THE `message`, WHERE THE GENERATOR IS UNREACHABLE — THE AUTHORING SPLIT, DECLARED AS A REQUIREMENT AND NOT AS AN INFERENCE, AND IT IS THE CLAUSE THE IMPLEMENTER'S `CF-1` ASKED FOR.** **THE PREMISE, STATED FIRST BECAUSE THE WHOLE CLAUSE FOLLOWS FROM IT: THE STORE'S GENERATOR IS REACHED ONLY THROUGH THE ADDON** — the artefact's own `errorMessage`, whose one caller is this package's absent/failed-addon step — **so EXACTLY WHERE THE ARTEFACT IS ABSENT OR PRESENT-BUT-UNLOADABLE, THE GENERATOR IS UNREACHABLE, because the thing that carries it is the thing that failed.** **THE SPLIT, IN TWO ARMS, AND EVERY STATE ABOVE LANDS IN ONE OF THEM:**
   - **(a) WHERE AN ARTEFACT LOADED FAR ENOUGH TO EXPOSE `errorMessage`, THE STORE'S OWN GENERATOR AUTHORS THE STRING.** **This is `present-wrong-version` — the rung-one artefact that loaded and answered `version` — and any state in which a loaded artefact carries the generator; the message is THAT generator's return value for `FilePathUnreachable` at the declared subject, and nothing beside it.**
   - **(b) WHERE NO ARTEFACT DID, THE ENTRY REPRODUCES THE GENERATOR'S OWN CLAUSE BYTE-FOR-BYTE AT THE DECLARED SUBJECT.** **This is `absent` and `present-unloadable`, at every key class, and the reproduction is the generator's clause, its subject slot included.**
   **AND THE RULE THAT MAKES THE REPRODUCTION LAWFUL — THREE PROPERTIES, EACH CHECKABLE, AND IT IS NOT A LOOPHOLE FOR A HAND-AUTHORED MESSAGE: (i) THE PACKAGE MINTS NO STATUS** — the status is the store's `FilePathUnreachable` member carried VERBATIM, as `§1.4` item 1 already fixes; **(ii) THE STRING IS THE STORE'S OWN CLAUSE AND NOT A PACKAGE-AUTHORED TEMPLATE** — a third spelling, an added word, a re-ordered clause or a second sentence beside it is a FINDING, and the string's equality with the generator's own rendering is a property of the SOURCE and is asserted by the cell that drives the state; **and (iii) ITS AUTHOR IS NAMED AS THE ARTEFACT THAT FAILED** — the entry reproduces the clause because the generator's author is the very artefact the load path could not load, so the reproduction is the generator's rendering of that code at that subject and is never the package speaking in the store's voice. **SO THE SINGLE-AUTHOR RULE IS NOT WEAKENED: ONE AUTHOR SPEAKS IN BOTH ARMS — the store's generator — and arm (b) is that author reached by its clause rather than by its function** (`PUB-DD-25`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §1.1 · the implementer's `CF-1` as the pass reports it, read with `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §2.5 item 2 — *"the message is the store's own GENERATED message VERBATIM — never hand-authored by the package"* | requirement]`]`).
   **AND THE RENDERING THIS CLAUSE OWES, RECONCILED TO THE ARTEFACT'S OWN AND NOT TO A PROSE SPELLING: THE BUILT ARTEFACT RENDERS THE SUBJECT QUOTED.** **The declared rendering is `'<subject>' is not reachable at construction, the path must exist or the application must be able to write it`** — the apostrophes around the subject are the GENERATOR'S OWN, supplied by the artefact's facade at the subject argument's spelling, **while the clause's prose above and `resolution.md §5.3` item 2 spell the subject UNQUOTED as the path or the paths.** **THE CONTRACT'S STATEMENT IS THE ARTEFACT'S RENDERING, because that is what a consumer SEES: the strings of both arms carry the apostrophes, and the equality arm (a) and arm (b) must satisfy is the equality of two strings that each carry them.** **SO THE UNQUOTED SPELLING IS READ AS A NAME FOR THE SLOT'S FILLING and NEVER as the slot's rendering — a message without the apostrophes is a FINDING, and `P-PUB-5`'s form check reads the rendering and not the prose** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-native/contract.md §8.2` — its `FilePathUnreachable` row prints the mismatch clause BESIDE the subject, and its `§8.2` example prints the subject quoted, `'<path>'`; read with `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §2.5 item 2 | cross-reference]`]`).
   **AND THE ARM THE VERSION READ ANSWERS WITH IS NOT THIS ONE, STATED SO THE SPLIT IS NOT READ AS WIDER THAN IT IS: where a substitution occurred the artefact LOADED, so the generator WAS reachable and the mismatch arm's string is authored by it — arm (a), at the rung-one artefact's own path as its subject.** `[A1 | state or fail-state declaration]`
7. **THE THIRD STATE THE TWO-ARM SPLIT DID NOT COVER, DECLARED HERE BECAUSE THE IMPLEMENTATION HAD TO ANSWER IT AND A CHOICE ANSWERED SILENTLY IS NOT A DECLARATION — THE LOADED ARTEFACT THAT CARRIES NO USABLE GENERATOR.** **THE STATE, STATED IN ONE SENTENCE: AN ARTEFACT LOADED AT RUNG ONE AND ITS OWN `version` DID NOT MATCH THE PACKAGE'S, AND THE SAME ARTEFACT'S `errorMessage` IS ABSENT, OR ITS OWN GENERATOR THROWS, OR IT ANSWERS A VALUE THAT IS NOT A `string` — so arm (a) has nothing to reach and the generator is UNREACHABLE THROUGH A FUNCTION though the artefact itself loaded.** **THIS IS A SUB-CASE OF ARM (b) AND NOT A THIRD ARM: the split remains TWO ARMS and nothing here widens it, because the property arm (b) is declared on — THE ENTRY REPRODUCES THE GENERATOR'S OWN CLAUSE BYTE-FOR-BYTE, MINTS NO STATUS, AND NAMES ITS AUTHOR AS THE ARTEFACT THAT FAILED — holds in all three of its trigger conditions, which are `absent`, `present-unloadable`, and this one.** **AND THE SUB-CASE'S SUBJECT RULE IS DECLARED BECAUSE `§1.4` ITEM 2 AND `§1.5` ITEM 6 GIVE TWO DIFFERENT SUBJECTS AND ONLY ONE OF THEM IS TRUE HERE: THE SUBJECT IS THE RUNG-ONE ARTEFACT'S OWN PATH ALONE** — the path the walk reached and the path this arm's own state is defined at — **and it is NEVER the both-paths join, because at this state ONLY RUNG ONE WAS TRIED:** the walk RETURNS at the arm and rung two's `require` is reached on no evaluation, so a message naming rung two's module-relative path would report a walk the loader did not take and would name as tried a path that was never tried. **A message at this state that appends the rung-two path, or that names the module-relative addon path in place of the rung-one artefact's own, is a FINDING** (`P-PUB-5`; `§1.5` item 5's disposition clause). **AND THE `alt`, PRICED BECAUSE A PINNED ANSWER OWES ONE: the state is answered by the second failure shape — an `Error`, or a package-authored token — rather than by arm (b)** — **priced: it gives the failure channel a second producer and a second shape, which `§1.4` item 1 and `P-PUB-5`'s failure list both forbid, and it makes a consumer whose artefact LOADED read a form a consumer whose artefact was ABSENT never meets; available with a fresh ruling recorded as an ACTIVE `docs/decisions.md` row, followed by the bounded amendment of this directory that `§0.3`'s reversal instrument names** (`PUB-DD-25`; `resolution.md §5.3` item 6; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §1.4` item 6 — the split this sub-case is filed under, and `P-PUB-4`'s failure list, which already names *"a mismatch arm whose `message` the ENTRY authored rather than the artefact's own generator"* | state or fail-state declaration]`).

### §1.5 The version read — `B-1`, its subject-narrowing, and the arm it answers to

1. **THE VERSION IS READ ON THE LOAD PATH, AT RUNG ONE, AND COMPARED WITH THE PACKAGE'S OWN VERSION.** **The addon answers its own version through the ONE name of its export set that exists for this purpose, called as a function and answering a `string`** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §1.3 item 4 — the addon's export set, `version` among the five, and its declared duty *"so the artefact can be audited from itself"* | cross-reference]`). **The package's own version is READ FROM THE PACKAGE'S OWN MANIFEST — the same artefact whose `version` the release's tag fixes — and never passed in, configured or defaulted.** (`P-PUB-4`; `PUB-DD-19`)
2. **A MISMATCH IS ANSWERED BY `§1.4`'s SINGLE DECLARED ARM, NEVER BY A FALLBACK.** **So a substituted artefact becomes a DECLARED REFUSAL: the package loads NOTHING from it, returns NO instance, and answers the one form with the artefact's own path as its subject.** **AND THE RUNG-TWO LOAD IS NOT COMPARED — `PUB-DD-18` states why and prices the alternative.** (`PUB-DD-17`; `PUB-DD-18`)
3. **THE SUBJECT-NARROWING THIS CLAUSE OWES, STATED WITH THE CELL IT NARROWS AND THE AMENDMENT THAT WILL LAND IT.** **`[M]` the JS unit's contract refuses a runtime ABI check in the package's own load path BY NAME — its words: *"A RUNTIME ABI CHECK IN THE PACKAGE'S OWN LOAD PATH IS REFUSED"*, because it *"would need a failure form the single form of `surface.md §2.5` does not admit"* (`docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md` `§1.4` item 3).** **THE READING THIS CONTRACT DECLARES, AND THE ONE THE AMENDMENT WILL WRITE INTO THAT CELL: that refusal's SUBJECT IS A PROBE OF THE RUNTIME'S ABI — an assertion about the HOST's Node-API version — while A VERSION READ IS NOT AN ABI CHECK: it reads THIS ARTEFACT'S OWN SELF-REPORT and asserts nothing about the runtime.** **SO THE TWO ARE NOT IN CONFLICT, AND THE CELL'S SUBJECT IS NARROWED TO WHAT IT REFUSED.** **AND THE NARROWING IS A `Q3` DUTY, NOT AN EDIT HERE: the cell is `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §1.4` item 3, it lies inside the `A82`+`A84` two-home amendment, and `§9` names it among the six cells that amendment carries** (`PUB-DD-*`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.2 · `AV-5` — the subject-narrowing's own condition, whose `[J]` half lands inside the two-home amendment | requirement]`).
4. **AND THE ONE CLAIM THIS CLAUSE DOES **NOT** MAKE, STATED SO IT IS NOT READ AS A WIDER MITIGATION: the read is NOT a DIGEST check, NOT a signature check and NOT a provenance check.** **`B-2` (a per-load digest) and `B-3` (npm's provenance and attestation) are PRICED AT THE FILING AND NOT ADOPTED as this unit's load-path fix** (`docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §8.2` · `B-2`/`B-3`, and `§8.3`'s three adopted). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §8.2` and `§8.3` — the priced options and the three adopted | prohibition]`
5. **THE INSTRUMENT IS NOT NEW AND IS NOT UNWIRED AFTER THIS UNIT: `[M]` the addon already exports its version and no caller on the load path uses it, so this clause WIRES an instrument that exists** (`[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §9 · `A84`(b) — the instrument exists and is unwired | cross-reference]`). `[A1 | cross-reference]`
6. **THE BOTH-PATHS SUBJECT'S JOIN — THE SEPARATOR, THE ORDER, AND THE ONE-SLOT RENDERING, DECLARED AS A REQUIREMENT AT `§1.4` item 2's own arm, AND IT IS THE CLAUSE THE IMPLEMENTER'S `CF-2` ASKED FOR.** **THE CLAUSE APPLIES EXACTLY WHERE `§1.4` item 2 FIXES THE TWO-PATH SUBJECT: where BOTH RUNGS WERE TRIED and neither yielded a loadable artefact, the declared absent form's subject NAMES BOTH PATHS.** **THE JOIN'S THREE PARTS ARE DECLARED, EACH ONCE:**
   - **(a) THE SEPARATOR IS `', '` — a comma and one space — AND NO OTHER SEPARATOR IS LAWFUL.**
   - **(b) THE ORDER IS RUNG ONE FIRST AND RUNG TWO SECOND — rung one's resolved name, then rung two's module-relative path — so a consumer reads the subjects IN THE ORDER THE LOAD PATH TRIED THEM.** **AND THE ORDER IS THE LOAD PATH'S OWN RATHER THAN A PRESENTATION CHOICE: `§1.1`'s order makes rung one's attempt precede rung two's, so a subject that reported them in any other order would state a walk the loader did not take.**
   - **(c) EACH PATH IS KEPT INSIDE THE GENERATOR'S OWN `'<subject>'` SLOT.** **AND THE ONE-SLOT RENDERING IS DECLARED, BECAUSE THE STORE'S GENERATOR DOES NOT ADMIT TWO SUBJECT SLOTS FOR THIS CODE: the `FilePathUnreachable` arm's clause carries ONE subject slot, so TWO SUBJECTS ARE RENDERED IN THE ONE SLOT BY MAPPING THE GENERATOR'S OWN RENDERING ACROSS THE TWO PATHS AND JOINING THE TWO RENDERINGS BY THE SEPARATOR — each path carrying its own apostrophes as `§1.4` item 6's rendering fixes them, and the clause's own subject slot then standing around the join.** **THE DECLARED RENDERING IS THEREFORE `''<rung one's resolved name>', '<rung two's module-relative path>'' is not reachable at construction, the path must exist or the application must be able to write it` — two quoted subjects inside the one slot, which is why the rendering opens with TWO apostrophes and closes with TWO: the first and last of them are the CLAUSE's own pair around the whole slot, and the two inner pairs are each subject's own.** **A rendering that quoted the joined pair once and the path once, that separated the two paths by any other token, or that reported them without the inner apostrophes is a FINDING** (`PUB-DD-26`).
   **AND THE READING THAT GOVERNS WHERE THE TWO READINGS OF THIS SUBJECT DISAGREE, STATED WITH THE ARTIFACT IT SETTLES.** **THIS CONTRACT'S BOTH-PATHS READING GOVERNS.** **THE RULE IS READ WITH ITS TERM: the subject names every subject THE LOAD PATH TRIED — both paths where both rungs were tried and neither yielded a loadable artefact, and rung two's path alone where rung one was never attempted at all, which is `resolution.md §2.2`'s uncovered-key cell and `resolution.md §5.3` item 2's own term *"the paths that were TRIED"*.** **AND SO THE CONTRADICTION IS NAMED AND SETTLED: `resolution.md §2.2`'s uncovered cell — *"rung two's module-relative path alone, because rung one was never attempted and has no path to report"* — is that term READ EXACTLY, and it STANDS; while the arm's rendering as the live battery's drive has it — one path, at a COVERED key whose rung one WAS attempted — is SUPERSEDED by this reading.** `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §1.4` item 2 — the both-paths requirement, read with `resolution.md §2.2` and `resolution.md §5.3` item 2 | requirement]`
   **AND THE SUPERSESSION ITSELF, WITH THE ARTIFACT NAMED AND ITS OWNER NAMED, SO THE RECONCILIATION HAS AN AUTHORITY TO CITE: THE LIVE BATTERY'S `LIVE-8.7` CELL — `bindings/js/greens/live-drive.mjs`'s absent-addon scenario, the cell whose declared expectation is that THE REFUSAL NAMES THE ABSENT ADDON'S OWN PATH as an EXACT STRING EQUALITY — IS THEREBY SUPERSEDED IN ITS SINGLE-PATH EXPECTATION.** **The battery's own key is COVERED (`linux` · `x64`), so rung one IS attempted and misses; both rungs were tried, so the subject is BOTH paths, and an expectation of the rung-two path alone is the superseded reading.** **The artifact's OWNER is `role_live_scenario_runner`, the battery is the sibling unit's live drive at its own record (`archive/gate6/storeroom-js-live-determination.md §5`, scenario `LIVE-8`, and its `§4` table row), and the reconciliation IS ANOTHER ROLE'S ACT RATHER THAN AN EDIT HERE: no byte under `bindings/js/**` and no archived record is written by this pass.** **AND THE REST OF `LIVE-8` IS NOT SUPERSEDED AND IS NOT MADE FALSE: `.1`–`.6` (the faithful copy, the entry's lazy resolution, the one form with `FilePathUnreachable` and no instance, the artefact's own path PRESENT IN the message, the second construction with nothing written, and the positive control) each hold under BOTH readings.** **AND THE UNCOVERED-KEY CELLS ARE NOT SUPERSEDED EITHER, AND THE REASON IS THE TERM ABOVE: an uncovered key's rung one is not attempted, so its subject is rung two's path alone, and the cells that read it so — `resolution.md §2.2`, `publish.test.mjs`'s uncovered-key drive, and this contract's own `§1.4` table's uncovered row — each HOLD.** `[A1 — the disposition is this contract's own declaration, and the artifact it supersedes is named rather than edited: `bindings/js/greens/live-drive.mjs`'s `LIVE-8.7`, owned by `role_live_scenario_runner`, being reconciled in parallel; read with `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §1.4` item 2 | ordering claim]`]`

### §1.7 The artefact-level ceiling the first workflow run met — the declared target matrix and the four builds that had never been attempted

**⟶ AND ONE CLAUSE IS ADDED AT THIS SECTION'S FOOT, 2026-10-12, BY THE `U-STOREROOM-CONSUMER-FIX` SPEC PASS (`role_spec_writer`), BECAUSE IT IS `§1.5`'S OWN SUBJECT AND MUST BE READ WITH IT.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **every clause of `§1` — `§1.1`'s three rungs and their order, `§1.2`'s total platform table, `§1.3`'s closed key set, `§1.4`'s four states/three classes/one arm with its `12`-cell arithmetic, `§1.5`'s version read (items `1`–`5`) and its both-paths join (item `6`, with its `LIVE-8.7` supersession) — STANDS BYTE-FOR-BYTE and is read THROUGH this clause; this clause adds ONE item to `§1.5`'s subject-matter, moves no rung, no state, no key, no name, no arm, no subject and no rendering, and writes no byte of the JS unit's directory.**

1. **`§1.5`'S VERSION READ IS SATISFIABLE BY CONSTRUCTION AT RUNG ONE FOR EVERY RELEASED ARTEFACT, AND THE CONSTRUCTION IS NAMED SO THE READ IS NOT READ AS A LUCKY COINCIDENCE: THE ARTEFACT'S OWN `version` IS STAMPED AT BUILD TIME FROM THE PACKAGE MANIFEST'S `version`, WITH `env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")` AS THE FALLBACK** — **so the equality item `1` compares is established BEFORE the comparison runs, and what the comparison can still refuse is a SUBSTITUTED artefact, an EMPTY self-report, a NON-STRING self-report, an UNCALLABLE `version`, a STALE stamp and an ABSENT-stamp fallback** (`docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §2.4`; `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §6.10`; `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §6.1`; `PAR-PUB-35`).
2. **AND THE CLAUSE'S OWN SUBJECT-NARROWING LIMB IS COMPLETED BECAUSE THIS IS THE CLAUSE THAT CARRIES IT: `§1.5` ITEM `3` NARROWS THE JS UNIT'S ABI-CHECK REFUSAL TO A PROBE OF THE RUNTIME'S ABI AND DECLARES THE VERSION READ AS ITS OWN, AND WHAT THAT DECLARATION LEAVES UNSTATED IS WHERE THE READ VALUE COMES FROM — WHICH THIS ITEM AND `§5.0a` NOW DECLARE, at THIS unit's home, WITHOUT EDITING `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §1.4` item `3`, whose narrowing remains a `Q3` duty of the two-home amendment** (`§9` · `Q3-1`).
3. **AND NOTHING OF THE ARM MOVES: the single declared failure arm's form (`{status, message}`), its status member, its subject and its terminality ACROSS EVALUATIONS are `§1.4` items `1`–`2`'s and `§5.5`'s, and this pass adds no case to the version read's own mismatch set beyond naming the fallback as a member of it** (`§1.4`; `§1.5` item `2`; `P-PUB-4`).
`[A2 docs/decisions.md · the ACTIVE `A91` row of 2026-10-12; A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §2.4` and `§6.10`; read with `§1.5`, `§1.4` and `§5.0a` | cross-reference]`

**AND THE CONTRACT DOCUMENT'S OWN HOME FOR `§1.5`'S NEW ITEM IS THIS CLAUSE, WHICH INTRODUCES NO `§`-LEVEL SECTION BECAUSE THIS PASS ADDS NONE: it is read with `release.md §2.4` (the decision, its price and the independence restatement), `release.md §6.10` (the directive's exact name, the absent case, the empty case, the validation question and the cache hazard), `§5.0a` (the register row `P-PUB-9`) and `§5.0b` (the guard row `P-PUB-10`).** **THE SIX FACTS THIS UNIT DECLARES ABOUT THE STAMP, EACH ONCE:** **(i)** **the directive's exact name is `STOREROOM_JS_PACKAGE_VERSION`; (ii)** **the carrier is the BUILD-SCRIPT FORM — `bindings/js/build.rs` reads the manifest itself — and NO ambient environment variable is consulted, so the workflow-env form is REFUSED; (iii)** **where the manifest cannot be read the stamp falls back to the crate's `CARGO_PKG_VERSION`, WHICH REPRODUCES THIS UNIT'S DEFECT AND IS LEGAL IN A DEVELOPMENT BUILD AND ILLEGAL IN A RELEASE BUILD, WITH THE ENFORCEMENT OUTSIDE THE CRATE — the crate reads no release flag, SO NOTHING IN IT ENFORCES THE DISTINCTION, and only the release's own pre-publish guard fails it; (iv)** **an EMPTY string is a value that was READ and NOT an absent manifest, so NO FALLBACK RUNS FROM IT; (v)** **the stamped value is NOT validated as a semver by any pass of this unit and the carrier must not add a third validator; and (vi)** **the carrier MUST declare a re-run trigger on the manifest it reads, because a warm `target/` otherwise re-emits a stale stamp and the artefact ships the PREVIOUS version** (`release.md §2.4` items `1`–`2`; `release.md §6.10` items `1`–`5`; `PAR-PUB-35`; `P-PUB-9`). `[A2 docs/decisions.md · the ACTIVE `A91` row of 2026-10-12; A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §2.4` and `§6.10`; A1 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §6.1 | declared parameter semantics]`

**EVERY CLAUSE OF `§1.1`–`§1.5` ABOVE STANDS BYTE-FOR-BYTE AND IS READ WITH THIS SECTION: THIS SECTION IS A PURE INSERTION, it adds no rung, no state, no key, no name and no arm, and it EDITS NO CELL ABOVE IT.** **Its subject is the ARTEFACT rather than the load path: what the five declared targets' BUILD must satisfy before any clause of `§1.1`–`§1.5` can be exercised at all.** `[A1 — the section is this document's own declaration; its evidence is the run's own log, quoted below | state or fail-state declaration]`

1. **THE FIVE-TARGET MATRIX WAS A DECLARATION WHOSE FOUR NON-LINUX BUILDS HAD NEVER BEEN ATTEMPTED — AND THIS CONTRACT STATES THAT PLAINLY, BECAUSE IT IS THE UNIT'S OWN CEILING MET AT THE ARTEFACT LEVEL.** **THE FIVE DECLARED TARGETS AND THEIR RUNNERS ARE UNMOVED (`§3.1` item 3; `release.md §1.2`), and NOT ONE OF THEM IS WITHDRAWN OR NARROWED BY THIS SECTION.** **WHAT THE SECTION RECORDS IS WHAT THE FIRST WORKFLOW RUN MEASURED: THE `linux` TARGETS LINK AND THE THREE NON-LINUX TARGETS' BUILDS DO NOT, BECAUSE THE EXPORT FACADE CALLS THE NODE-API C FUNCTIONS DIRECTLY AND THE NODE HOST SUPPLIES THEM AT LOAD TIME — and a host-supplied symbol is a MISSING symbol to the two linkers whose reach the `linux` target's loader does not share.** **THE TWO FAILURES, TRANSCRIBED FROM THE RUN'S OWN LOG AT THEIR OWN LINES, EACH AS THE RUN PRINTED IT:**
   - **`darwin-arm64`:** ***"`_napi_set_named_property`, referenced from: …facade…Codec::set_member"*** **→** ***"`ld: symbol(s) not found for architecture arm64`"***.
   - **`win32-x64`:** ***"`LNK2019: unresolved external symbol napi_set_element … `"*** **→** ***"`LNK1120: 23 unresolved externals`"***.
   **AND THE MECHANISM IS ONE SENTENCE, STATED SO THE SECTION IS A REQUIREMENT RATHER THAN A REPORT: A `linux` SHARED OBJECT IS PERMITTED TO CARRY UNDEFINED SYMBOLS THAT ITS LOADER RESOLVES AT LOAD TIME, while macOS's `ld` and Windows' MSVC REFUSE TO EMIT A BUNDLE CARRYING THEM.** **SO THE `linux` DECLARATION WAS TRUE OF `linux` AND THE OTHER FOUR WERE DECLARATIONS WITHOUT A BUILD BEHIND THEM.** **AND ONE CONSEQUENCE OF THAT CEILING IS NAMED BECAUSE IT IS THE SHARP EDGE OF IT: THE TESTS THAT WOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT COULD NOT LINK ON THE VERY TARGETS THEY WERE MEANT TO COVER** — a `cargo test` for `darwin-arm64` or `win32-x64` meets the same unresolved symbols the artefact's own build meets, so the matrix's own tests could not have distinguished a broken build from a missing one. `[A1 — the run's own log, quoted verbatim at its own lines; read with `release.md §6` item 3 | state or fail-state declaration]`
2. **THE REQUIREMENT, PER TARGET, FIXED ONCE — AND IT IS DECLARED AT `distribution.md §7`, WHERE THE FIVE ARTEFACTS' BUILD BELONGS.** **THE REQUIREMENT IS THAT EVERY DECLARED TARGET'S BUILD PRODUCE A LOADABLE BUNDLE, WHICH THE NODE HOST CAN SATISFY AT LOAD TIME:** **macOS** — the `-undefined dynamic_lookup` LINK FLAG; **Windows MSVC** — THE NODE IMPORT LIBRARY, AT A DECLARED PINNED VERSION; **`linux`** — NOTHING, DECLARED AS A NO-OP, because the `linux` loader needs no flag and that is the honest statement of what `linux` needs. **THE REQUIREMENT'S FORM IS A CLAUSE OF THE `distribution` PART (`distribution.md §7`) AND ITS CARRYING FILE IS THE SIBLING CRATE'S (`bindings/js/build.rs`), NAMED AT `§9`'s duty table with the amendment that declares it.** **AND A BUILD-SURFACE FILE IS DECLARED RATHER THAN DISCOVERED: the declaration, the rule it satisfies and the surface it joins are at `distribution.md §7` item 3, and `P-PUB-7`'s failure list carries the observable.** `[A1 — the requirement is this section's own declaration; its subject is the artefact's build and not the load path; read with `distribution.md §7` and `§9` · `Q3-12` | requirement]`
3. **AND NO CLAUSE OF THIS SECTION MOVES A KEY, A NAME, A RUNG, A STATE, A FAILURE SHAPE OR A FIGURE OF `§1.1`–`§1.5`.** **THE FIVE-NAME SET STAYS CLOSED AT FIVE (`§1.3`); the three rungs and their order are unmoved (`§1.1`); the platform table's totality is unmoved (`§1.2`); the four states, the `4 × 3` matrix's `11`-answered-and-`1`-unreachable arithmetic and the single arm are unmoved (`§1.4`); and the version read, its exact-equality predicate and its subject-narrowing are unmoved (`§1.5`).** **A SIXTH TARGET IS STILL A BOUNDED AMENDMENT AND NOT A BUILD FLAG (`§2.2`; `PUB-DD-8`), and a build-script pin is NOT a target and is NOT a sixth name.** `[A1 | prohibition]`

---

**⟶ RECONCILED 2026-10-11 — THE STAGED-RELEASE PASS (`role_spec_writer`), ON THE SECOND WORKFLOW RUN'S OWN EVIDENCE AS ITS BRIEF CARRIES IT: `§3.1`'s SIX STEPS BECOME A TWO-PHASE SEQUENCE — `stage`, THEN THE ARCHITECT'S OWN PROMOTION — AND THE REGISTRY'S REFUSAL IS KEPT APART FROM THE TWO EARLIER DEFECTS THAT WERE OURS.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **every byte of `§1.1`–`§1.7` above STANDS BYTE-FOR-BYTE and is read THROUGH this clause; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION, it adds no rung, no state, no key, no name, no arm and no register row, and it renumbers no `§`-number.**
**THE CLAUSE THAT CARRIES THE TWO PHASES WHOLE IS `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §1.5` AND `§1.6`: `§1.5`'s `npm stage publish` AT BOTH PUBLISH SITES WITH THE ARCHITECT'S PROMOTION AS THE ACT THAT MAKES A STAGED VERSION LIVE, AND `§1.6`'s PROPERTY — A STAGED VERSION IS NOT SPENT UNTIL PROMOTED, SO A BAD STAGE IS DISCARDED RATHER THAN DEPRECATED.** **THIS CLAUSE STATES THE TWO THINGS `§1.1`–`§1.7` ABOVE NEED FROM IT AND NOTHING BESIDE THEM:**
1. **THE ARTEFACT'S OWN BUILD IS UNAFFECTED BY THE VERB.** **`§1.7`'s per-target link requirement, the pinned Node version and the `bindings/js/build.rs` declaration are facts of WHICH BYTES A STAGE CARRIES and not of HOW they reach the registry; the STAGE PHASE'S ONLY NEW OBLIGATION AT THIS SECTION'S OWN SUBJECT IS THAT THE BYTES ARE ASSEMBLED AND GUARDED BEFORE THEY ARE STAGED** (`§1.7`; `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/distribution.md §7`; `release.md §1.5`).
2. **AND THE ORDER PROPERTY THIS SECTION'S `§1.1`–`§1.4` DEPEND ON IS PRESERVED THROUGH BOTH PHASES: THE FIVE PLATFORM ARTEFACTS ARE STAGED AND PROMOTED FIRST AND THE MAIN PACKAGE IS STAGED AND PROMOTED LAST, SO THE MAIN PACKAGE'S FIVE EXACT PINS RESOLVE BEFORE IT LANDS.** **THE RECOMMENDED READING IS `release.md §1.5` ITEM `4` READING (A) — STAGE-AND-PROMOTE-THE-FIVE, THEN STAGE-AND-PROMOTE-THE-MAIN — AND THE ALTERNATIVE, STAGE-ALL-SIX-THEN-PROMOTE-IN-ORDER, IS PRICED THERE RATHER THAN LEFT UNSTATED** (`§1.1`; `§1.3` item `4`; `release.md §1.5` item `4`). `[A1 — the staged-release model is this pass's declaration on the run's own evidence; read with `§3.1`, `release.md §1.5`–`§1.6` and `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/distribution.md §7` | requirement]`

**⟶ AND THE RUN'S OWN RECORD, AT THIS DOCUMENT'S HOME, SO THE TWO FALSIFICATIONS AND THE ONE REFUSAL ARE READ AS THREE CLASSES AND NOT AS ONE** (`RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **this clause is a PURE INSERTION, it moves no row, mints no id and re-decides no rule of `§1.7` above**): **THE SECOND WORKFLOW RUN's verbatim lines are carried WHOLE at `release.md §6.7`, and this clause names only what each line settles.**
1. **WHAT WORKED, AND IT WAS THE HARD PART: THE OIDC EXCHANGE AND THE PROVENANCE ATTESTATION BOTH SUCCEEDED.** **The three lines, verbatim: *"`npm notice Publishing to https://registry.npmjs.org/ with tag latest and public access`"* · *"`npm notice publish Signed provenance statement with source and build information from GitHub Actions`"* · *"`npm notice publish Provenance statement published to transparency log: https://search.sigstore.dev/?logIndex=3195991700`"*.** **SO `release.md §1.4`'s `setup-node` STEP AND THE PINNED-CLIENT FIX ARE THE RELEASE'S FIRST VERIFIED WORKING PIECE, and the first run's `ENEEDAUTH` is ANSWERED by the floor clause's own remedy** (`release.md §1.4`'s CI-inputs clause; `release.md §6.5`).
2. **WHAT THE REGISTRY REFUSED, AND ITS NATURE: *"`npm error code E403`"* · *"`403 Forbidden - PUT …/@auspicion%2fprovident-storeroom-js-linux-x64-gnu - OIDC permission denied for this action`"*.** **AND THE NATURE MUST BE RECORDED AS IT IS: THIS IS NOT A DEFECT OF THE CODE, OF THE WORKFLOW OR OF THE PROVENANCE.** **The trusted-publisher grant configured for these names is scoped to STAGE PUBLISH rather than to DIRECT PUBLISH, so the direct verb is refused BY THE GRANT — and the answer is a VERB, declared at `release.md §1.5`** (`§3.1` item `6`; `release.md §6.7` item `2`).
3. **AND IT IS KEPT APART FROM THE TWO EARLIER FALSIFICATIONS, WHICH WERE OURS.** **`§1.7`'s LINK DEFECT — the four non-`linux` builds never attempted — WAS OURS, and its remedy is `distribution.md §7`'s per-target link requirement with `bindings/js/build.rs` as its carrying file.** **`release.md §6.5`'s CLIENT FLOOR — an `npm` below `11.5.1` that cannot perform the exchange — WAS ALSO OURS, and its remedy is the `setup-node` CI input.** **THIS RUN'S REFUSAL IS NEITHER: it is a permission the registry did not grant, so THE THREE ARE THREE CLASSES AND ARE READ AS THREE — two ours and repaired, one the registry's own scope and answered by the staged model** (`§1.7`; `release.md §6.7` item `3`).
4. **AND ONE FINDING THAT IS OURS, RECORDED AT ITS OWN HOME SO IT IS NOT MERGED WITH THE LINK DEFECT: THE SIBLING CRATE'S OWN SUITE HAD ONLY EVER COMPILED ON `unix`.** **The second run's `win32-x64` leg could not compile `bindings/js/tests/crossing_carriers.rs` — `std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt` and `Permissions::from_mode` — while `release.md §1.4`'s step `1` runs that suite on every declared target's own runner.** **IT IS THE SAME CLASS AS `§1.7`'s LINK DEFECT — a coverage claim wider than the platform it was ever exercised on — and `§5.7` below carries its observable; the remedy and its price are at `release.md §7.2`** (`§1.7` item `1`; `release.md §7`). `[A1 — the run's own log, quoted verbatim at its own lines at `release.md §6.7`; the classification is this clause's own declaration | state or fail-state declaration]`

**AND ONE FIGURE THIS INSERTION MOVES, PRINTED WITH ITS TERMS SO IT IS NOT READ FROM A DIFF: THE `PUB-DD` FAMILY MOVES `30 → 31` ROWS, because this pass inserts `PUB-DD-31` — the promotion verb's exact spelling and who supplies it — at `release.md §1.6`'s foot, so `30 + 1 = 31` ✓ and the family's OPERATIVE range is `PUB-DD-1`…`PUB-DD-31`.** **EVERY RANGE OR COUNT CELL THAT PRINTS `30`, `29`, `28`, `26` OR `24` IS READ AS THE COUNT AT ITS OWN PASS'S AUTHORING — this document's index region, `§0.2`'s id-families table, `§0.3`'s four arithmetic clauses, `§5.0`'s count-disclosure clause and `§9`'s duty arithmetic included — and NO PRE-PASS ROW IS MOVED, DROPPED OR RENUMBERED.** `[A1 | numeric or census claim]`

## §2. The distribution — this document's own statement, carried whole by the `distribution` part


**AUTHORITY NOTE FOR THIS SECTION.** **This section's clauses are AUTHORED HERE and carried, clause for clause, at `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/distribution.md`, the `carrier` part this directory's map declares for it**; **a citation to either home names its path plus the `§`-number.** `[A1 | cross-reference]`

### §2.1 What the main package ships — `A85`(1)

1. **`build/Release` LEAVES `files`.** **The main package's allowlist is `index.mjs` · `README.md` · `index.d.ts` and NOTHING ELSE; NO binary ships, at any key, on any target** (`PUB-DD-1`; `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §1.3 item 1 — the one arms-bearing entry the allowlist must carry | requirement]`).

**AND THE MAIN PACKAGE'S OWN LICENCE COPY HAS A DECLARED CARRIAGE, STATED IN THIS CLAUSE SO THIS ALLOWLIST AND `§2.3` ITEM 2 READ AS ONE DECLARATION AND NOT AS TWO CELLS THAT DISAGREE.** **`LICENSE` IS NOT AN ALLOWLIST ENTRY AND IS NOT A THREE-ENTRY LIST'S FOURTH MEMBER; the main package's licence copy is landed by THE RELEASE'S OWN ASSEMBLY STEP, which copies the repository's `LICENSE` byte-for-byte into EACH of the six artefacts' tarballs** — **the same step and the same mechanism the five platform artefacts' copies are landed by, declared at `distribution.md §4.3` item 2 and owed as the `Q3-5` duty.** **SO THE TWO CELLS AGREE EXACTLY AS THEY STAND AND NEITHER IS AMENDED: `§2.1` item 1 fixes WHAT THE MANIFEST DECLARES, and `§2.3` item 2 fixes WHAT THE ARTEFACT CARRIES; the copy is a fact of the TARBALL rather than a fourth entry of the allowlist, which is why `PUB-DD-11` already reads the per-artefact copy as *"the licence's TEXT travelling with an artefact"* and not as a fifth declaration site.** **AND THE CONSEQUENCE IS NAMED SO IT IS NOT DISCOVERED: a consumer who unpacks the tarball reads FOUR published paths — the three allowlist rows AND the `LICENSE` copy the assembly step added — while the tracked manifest's `files` array names THREE, and that difference is the mechanism rather than a defect.** **AND THE TWO READINGS THAT WOULD RECONCILE THE CELLS OTHERWISE ARE EACH REFUSED: adding a `LICENSE` entry to the main package's `files` is REFUSED because it would contradict `§2.1` item 1 on its own face, and narrowing `§2.3` item 2 to exclude the main package is REFUSED because the copy is required to travel with the main artefact exactly as it travels with the five.** **The `alt` is available with a fresh ruling recorded as an ACTIVE `docs/decisions.md` row, followed by the bounded amendment of this directory that `§0.3`'s reversal instrument names** (`PUB-DD-11`; `PUB-DD-12`; `§2.3` item 2; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/contract.md §0.3 · `PUB-DD-11`'s reversal cell | declared parameter semantics]`).

2. **RUNG TWO IS THE CHECKOUT-ONLY RUNG IT ALREADY IS.** **In a published install rung two resolves to a path that a `files`-restricted tarball does not carry, so rung one carries every covered consumer and rung two carries the in-place developer loop and every battery that exists today** (`§1.1`; `PUB-DD-1`).
3. **THE UNCOVERED-TARGET ANSWER IS A DECLARED LIMITATION, WITH ITS CONSEQUENCE IN THE SHIPPED `README.md` SECTION.** **A consumer on an uncovered key gets NO binary, NO buildable route from the tarball, and NO `build.rs`, `Cargo.toml` or `src/**`; their only route is to build the store crate from its source themselves** (`PUB-DD-2`; `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.3 item 3 — the consumer's toolchain requirement declared rather than discovered, cited as the clause this unit's README section extends | requirement]`).
4. **THE SOURCE ARCHIVE IS PRICED AND NOT ADOPTED: where the uncovered-target answer is wanted actionable, the store crate's source is a SEPARATE, NON-REGISTRY distribution with its own provenance subject, and NOTHING of it enters the npm tarball or the registry under any of the six names** (`PUB-DD-2`; `PUB-DD-22`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §11 · `A85`(1) — the default and its named mitigation | requirement]`

### §2.2 The five platform artefacts — one per target, each installable on its own

**THE FIVE PLATFORM ARTEFACTS ARE DELIVERABLES RATHER THAN OPTIONS, AND EACH IS SPECIFIED FIELD BY FIELD AT `distribution.md §2`, whose table is the operative one.** **AND ONE CLAUSE GOVERNS THE SET, PRINTED HERE SO THE MANIFESTS ARE READ WITH IT: a further target is a BOUNDED AMENDMENT and NOT a build flag, so a sixth platform directory, a sixth manifest and a sixth name are each a FINDING** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.2 · `DD-25` — the matrix's membership and the bounded-amendment rule, cited at its own home and never amended here | prohibition]`). **NO BINARY IS EVER COMMITTED TO THE TRACKED TREE**, so each platform directory carries its manifest and no `.node` (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.2 — the no-committed-binary prohibition | prohibition]`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §9 · `A84`(a) — the five scoped artefacts | numeric or census claim]`

### §2.3 The licence notice and its six artefacts

1. **THE DISTRIBUTION'S DECLARED FACT IS `AGPL-3.0`, DECLARED AT FOUR SITES, AND THE FOURTH'S SPELLING CARRIES THE `-or-later` CLAUSE** — `(i)` the main package's manifest `license` field, `AGPL-3.0`; `(ii)` `bindings/js/README.md`, `AGPL-3.0`; `(iii)` `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.4`, `AGPL-3.0`; and `(iv)` the sibling crate's own `bindings/js/Cargo.toml` `license` field, `AGPL-3.0-or-later`. **THE DIVERGENCE IS DECLARED AND NOT REPAIRED** (`PUB-DD-12`; `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.4 item 3 — the four sites and the two spellings, at their own home | cross-reference]`).
2. **THE TARBALL'S OWN COPY IS AN ARTEFACT-LEVEL FACT.** **Each of the SIX artefacts — the main package and the five platform artefacts — carries a copy of the repository's own `LICENSE` as the licence TEXT travelling with it** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.4 item 2 — the registry artefact carries its own copy | requirement]`; `PUB-DD-11`).
3. **EACH OF THE FIVE PLATFORM ARTEFACTS' OWN NOTICE IS ANSWERED RATHER THAN ASSUMED, AND THE ANSWER IS YES: each platform tarball carries the copy and its manifest's own `license` field reads `AGPL-3.0`** — **and the per-artefact copy is NOT a FIFTH DECLARATION SITE under `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §3.4` item 3, because that item's list is a list of AUTHORED DECLARATIONS ABOUT the licence while a tarball's own copy is the licence's text travelling with an artefact** (`PUB-DD-11`; the union's row `4`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §3.2 · the row `4` carriage — the five artefacts' own notices, answered five at once | requirement]`).
4. **`bindings/js/LICENSE` IS REFUSED.** **`[M]` no such file exists in the tree, and none is written by any pass: the refusal is `DD-43`'s and it stands unreversed** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.2 · `DD-43` — the licence file itself is NOT copied into the sibling crate | prohibition]`; `PUB-DD-12`).
5. **AND ONE CLAUSE ON THE APPENDIX, SINCE THE DELIVERABLE ENUMERATES IT: the AGPL appendix's programme-name line is `Provident-Storeroom` and its copyright line is `Copyright (C) 2026 Auspicion Innovations LLC`, both the architect's own acts; and NO clause of this unit changes the repository's `LICENSE`** (`PUB-DD-12`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §3 item 4 and `§9`'s provenance, where the licence acts are named as his own commits | cross-reference]`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §4 item 3 — the licence dispositions re-decided rather than inherited | declared parameter semantics]`

### §2.4 The types — `A85`(2), a union over a declared-but-open set

1. **THE `.d.ts` IS AUTHORED, NOT GENERATED.** **It is a TRACKED, hand-authored file at `bindings/js/index.d.ts`, listed in the main package's `files` and exposed through its `types` field** (`PUB-DD-3`; `PUB-DD-21`).
2. **ITS `status` IS A UNION OVER A DECLARED-BUT-OPEN SET.** **The fifteen members are DISCOVERABLE in the type AND an unknown status is still typed** — the union's members beside an explicit escape — **so the type survives a ruling that moves the store's set, and the set's MEMBERSHIP is declared in the type's own prose as THE STORE'S and not the package's** (`PUB-DD-3`).
3. **THE CLOSED SET'S MEMBERSHIP IS THE STORE'S, AND THIS UNIT MINTS NO MEMBER, WIDENS NO SET AND RENAMES NONE** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-native/contract.md §8.1 — the closed `15`, whose membership is the store contract's to close | cross-reference]`).
4. **AND THE GENERATOR IS RECORDED AS A LATER OPTION AND NOT ADOPTED: a generated `.d.ts` would add a build-time dependency the sibling crate deliberately does not carry, it is priced at `PUB-DD-3`, and the sibling unit's own plan's build-surface row is NAMED AS A `Q3` DUTY rather than amended here** (`§9`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.2 · `AV-14` — the authored-not-generated condition and the plan-row duty | requirement]`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §11 · `A85`(2) — the default as adopted | declared parameter semantics]`

### §2.5 The metadata — declared absent rather than guessed, and supplied where no remote is needed

1. **`repository` · `homepage` · `bugs` ARE DECLARED ABSENT.** **`[M]` this repository carries no remote this project's own records name, so an immutable manifest field naming a path that does not resolve is forbidden by the project's own citation rule; the three fields are therefore ABSENT from the manifest and their absence is the declaration** (`PUB-DD-7`).
2. **`keywords`, a long `description` and `author` ARE SUPPLIED WITHOUT A REMOTE** (`PUB-DD-7`; `distribution.md §5`).
3. **AND THE `description` IS A LONG ONE, STATED SO THE FIELD'S SHAPE IS CHECKABLE RATHER THAN PREFERRED: it names the unit, the deliverable and the binding's own route, in the form the existing manifest's own description already uses.**
4. **NO SECRET, TOKEN, CREDENTIAL OR REGISTRY AUTH MATERIAL IS WRITTEN INTO ANY TRACKED BYTE OF THIS UNIT** (`PUB-DD-7`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §11 · `A85`(6) — the metadata half of the sixth default | declared parameter semantics]`

**⟶ RECONCILED 2026-10-11 — THE METADATA-REVERSAL PASS (`role_spec_writer`), ON THE REGISTRY'S OWN `E422` AND ON `PUB-DD-7`'s OWN PRE-AUTHORIZED REVERSAL COLUMN: `repository` · `homepage` · `bugs` ARE DECLARED, AND THEY CARRY THE REMOTE THE REGISTRY NAMED.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **item `1` above stands BYTE-FOR-BYTE and is read THROUGH this clause; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION — it adds no item, mints no id, renumbers no `§`, moves no register row and writes no byte outside `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/` and `docs/specs/storeroom-js/`.** **THE AS-FILED WORDS THIS CLAUSE READS FORWARD, RE-EMITTED SO THE MOVEMENT IS READABLE WITHOUT A DIFF: item `1` reads *"`repository` · `homepage` · `bugs` ARE DECLARED ABSENT. `[M]` this repository carries no remote this project's own records name, so an immutable manifest field naming a path that does not resolve is forbidden by the project's own citation rule; the three fields are therefore ABSENT from the manifest and their absence is the declaration"*, and the clause whose own column pre-authorized the movement is `PUB-DD-7`'s reversal cell — *"any host, one line; **the three metadata fields are written the moment a remote exists**"*.**

**WHY THE DECISION IS THE REGISTRY'S AND NOT A PREFERENCE: the registry's provenance verification REQUIRES `repository.url` TO MATCH THE PROVENANCE'S SOURCE REPOSITORY.** **The staged run's own `422` is the measurement, quoted verbatim: *"Error verifying sigstore provenance bundle: Failed to validate repository information: package.json: `\"repository.url\"` is `\"\"`, expected to match `\"https://github.com/LittleKingsguard/Provident-Storeroom\"` from provenance."*** **SO THE FIELD IS NOT A PREFERENCE — IT IS A PRECONDITION OF A STAGED PUBLISH, and the package that declares it absent cannot be staged at all while the provenance's subject is a source repository at all.** **AND THE STATE IS THE MECHANISM WORKING RATHER THAN A DECISION OVERTURNED: `PUB-DD-7`'s own reversal column PRICED THIS EXACT MOVEMENT — one line, *"the moment a remote exists"* — so the row's reversal has FIRED ON ITS OWN TERM, the absent limb is exhausted, and the operative value is the row's own alternative.**

### §3.2 The tag/version rule and the five artefacts' coupling

1. **VERSION EQUALS TAG.** **The tag's form is `v<major>.<minor>.<patch>`, the MAIN package's `version` is that tag's version, and NO publication happens from an untagged commit** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/contract.md §0.2 · `DD-42` — the publish step's trigger and versioning policy, cited at its own home | requirement]`).
2. **THE FIVE ARTEFACTS' COUPLING RULE IS THIS UNIT'S OWN AND IT IS ONE RULE: the five platform artefacts' `version` equals the MAIN package's `version`, the main package's `optionalDependencies` pins the five at EXACTLY that version, and the five are published BEFORE the main package** (`PUB-DD-10`; `P-PUB-8`).

**⟶ RECONCILED 2026-10-11 — THE RELEASE-VERSION PASS (`role_spec_writer`), ON `A89` (§15): THE VERSION ITEM'S FIGURE IS RE-EMITTED AT ITS AS-FILED READING AND RE-READ AT `0.1.1`, WITH `0.1.0` NAMED AS THE BOOTSTRAP VERSION — AND THE COUPLING RULE IS PRINTED WHERE THE VERSION IS DECLARED.** `RCA-8(d)` ANNOTATE-BESIDE: **items `1`, `2` and `3` above stand BYTE-FOR-BYTE and are read THROUGH this clause; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION — it adds no item, adds no register row, mints no id, renumbers no `§` and re-decides no rule.**

this clause adds no item to them, moves no figure and re-decides no limb of the coupling rule.** **THE LIMB, RE-EMITTED VERBATIM FROM ITEM `3`: *"the artefact's self-report's own source is a fact of `bindings/js/src/**`, which no clause of this unit declares"*.** **IT REMAINS TRUE OF THE CRATE'S `version` FIELD AND OF `bindings/js/Cargo.toml`: THE CRATE'S VERSION STAYS DELIBERATELY INDEPENDENT, the decision is not reversed, and no clause of this unit writes a byte of that manifest** (`release.md §2.2`'s independence clause, UNREVERSED). **AND IT IS NO LONGER TRUE, IN ITS OWN WORDS, OF THE **RELEASED SELF-REPORT**: UNDER THE STAMP — `A91`'s `(a2)` — the released `version()`'s source is **THE PACKAGE MANIFEST AT BUILD TIME**, carried through the directive `STOREROOM_JS_PACKAGE_VERSION`, so the self-report IS a thing this unit now declares, at `release.md §2.4`, `release.md §6.10` and `§5.0a`.** **SO THE CLAUSE IS TRUE BUT INCOMPLETE WITH THE STAMP LANDED, AND THE COMPLETION IS STATED BY NAME RATHER THAN LEFT TO A READER: THE CRATE'S VERSION IS INDEPENDENT AND UNDECLARED HERE; THE RELEASED SELF-REPORT IS STAMPED FROM THE PACKAGE MANIFEST AND IS DECLARED HERE** (`PAR-PUB-35`; `P-PUB-9`; `docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §6.1`). **AND `Q3-1`'S RECONCILIATION IS THEREBY DISCHARGED IN KIND RATHER THAN IN PLACE: its two priced forms — one line at the crate's own manifest, or one line at the artefact's own self-report — are SUPERSEDED BY THE STAMP, WHICH IS NEITHER, and the `Q3` duty table's own cell stands as the DUTY (the artefact's self-report must answer the package version) with a THIRD route now declared** (`§9` · `Q3-1`). `[A2 docs/decisions.md · the ACTIVE `A91` row of 2026-10-12; A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish/release.md §2.4`; read with `§3.2` item `2`, `§1.5` and `release.md §2.2` | declared parameter semantics]`

### §3.3 The partial-publish arm — one clause, both windows

1. **THE MAIN PACKAGE IS PUBLISHED LAST, AND THAT ORDER IS THE ARM'S PRECONDITION RATHER THAN A CONVENIENCE** (`PUB-DD-5`).
2. **A FAILURE BEFORE THE MAIN PACKAGE IS RECOVERED IN PLACE ON THE SAME VERSION.** **`k` platform artefacts have landed and the main package has not, so NOTHING A CONSUMER CAN INSTALL IS INCOMPLETE — because no main package exists to install — and the missing `6 − k` are published late against the SAME tag and the SAME version** (`PUB-DD-5`).
3. **A FAILURE AFTER THE MAIN PACKAGE IS ANSWERED BY DEPRECATE-AND-RE-PUBLISH AT THE NEXT PATCH.** **The tag is RE-CUT as `v0.1.1`, the six artefacts publish anew, and the half-published `0.1.0` is DEPRECATED with a message naming its replacement** (`PUB-DD-5`).
4. **AND THE ONE THING THIS CLAUSE REFUSES, STATED SO IT IS NOT ATTEMPTED: an UNPUBLISH of a published version as the PRIMARY arm, and an in-place recovery of the MAIN package.** **npm versions are immutable, so neither is available; and the arm spends a version only when nothing else can work** (`PUB-DD-5`; `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §10.1 · `Q-4`(a) with (b) as its precondition — the recommendation the default adopted | state or fail-state declaration]`).
5. **A RELEASE ATTEMPTED WITH NO DECLARED ARM IS A STOP CONDITION, NOT A RISK** (`implementation-plan.md §5` · stop condition `J`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §11 · `A85`(4) | state or fail-state declaration]`

### §3.4 The release acts and their actor

1. **NO PASS PUBLISHES, PACKS, TAGS, INSTALLS OR ATTESTS.** **The release acts are the ARCHITECT'S, and a pass's ceiling is the contract, the red set, the green, the adversarial pass, the blind greens, the doc gates, the trio, and the record that asks for his act** (`[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal.md §6 item 4 — *"THE RELEASE ITSELF REMAINS THE ARCHITECT'S ACT"* | prohibition]`).
2. **THE ACTS, ENUMERATED SO THE RECORD THAT ASKS FOR THEM IS CHECKABLE:** claiming the six names · creating the tag · publishing the five platform artefacts and then the main package, in that order · the provenance attestation · running the `LIVE/INSTALL` battery against the released bytes · deciding the partial-publish arm if `k` of six lands · and the licence line's final reading. `[A1 | requirement]`
3. **AND THE ONE ACT THAT IS NOT A RELEASE ACT AND IS STILL NOT A PASS'S: THE LEDGER MOVE AT THE CLOSE-OUT IS HIS ADMISSION** (`PUB-DD-4`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §6 item 1 | ordering claim]`

## §4. The acceptance layer `LIVE/INSTALL` — this document's own statement, carried whole by the `acceptance` part

**AUTHORITY NOTE FOR THIS SECTION.** **This section's clauses are AUTHORED HERE and carried, clause for clause, at `docs/specs/storeroom-publish/acceptance.md`, the `carrier` part this directory's map declares for it**; **a citation to either home names its path plus the `§`-number.** `[A1 | cross-reference]`

### §4.1 `LIVE/INSTALL`, defined in one line

**THE INSTALL OF THE RELEASED BYTES, BY A PACKAGE MANAGER, IN A CLEAN DIRECTORY, EXERCISED THROUGH THE CLAIMED ESM ENTRY TO A FINAL RESOLVED STATE — ADDITIVE, `APP` STILL `NONE`, AND NEVER A SUBSTITUTE FOR THIS UNIT'S OWN NODE LEGS.** `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.1 item 5 — the layer as the gate-1 record determines it, and `archive/gate1/storeroom-publish-review/3-architecture.md §A.4`'s table carried whole | requirement]`

### §4.2 Its instrument, its site and its label

1. **INSTRUMENT:** a **real package manager** operating on a **packed or released tarball** in a directory **with no repository in its ancestry and no `build/Release` beside the entry**, plus **one real Node process**.
2. **SITE:** **named per figure** — the clean directory's path class, the pack/install command, and the repository root the bytes were produced at.
3. **LABEL:** **`LIVE/INSTALL`**, this unit's own, declared here and cited (never inserted) at the sibling directory's layer clause; **NOT an `APP`-green, and `APP` remains `NONE`.** **The sibling unit's layer vocabulary is CITED AND NEVER EDITED: a layer-vocabulary amendment is not one of the two homes the amendment reaches** (`§9`; `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/acceptance.md §4.2 — the sibling LAYER label, cited and never re-labelled | prohibition]`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.1 item 5 | declared parameter semantics]`

### §4.3 What it verifies, and what it must NOT claim

1. **WHAT IT VERIFIES:** that the released bytes install at all; that the claimed ESM entry resolves after install; **that rung one — the platform package — is the rung that fires in a published install, which no in-place battery can reach**; that the version read does not refuse the released artefact; and that the declared failure arm is what an uncovered target meets.
2. **WHAT IT MUST NOT CLAIM, EACH WITH ITS REASON:** **not an `APP` figure** (the assembled layer for every unit of this project is `NONE`); **not a re-measurement of the sibling unit's own figures** (its `113` JS cells, its `240` blind assertions and its `91` live assertions are `ENVELOPE/PURE` figures of ANOTHER unit's suite, and this battery drives one install of released bytes and one Node process); **not a validation of any consuming application** (`HO-1`…`HO-7` remain handoffs and no consumer is wired by any pass); **not a proof that no consumer has ever loaded the package** (no instrument in this topology can falsify that universal negative, so it stays a `STATEMENT` — `PUB-DD-14`); **and not a re-labelling of the store's own figure** (the store's `379` is the store unit's and is not this unit's, at any spelling). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.1 item 5; read with `[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/acceptance.md §4.2 items 1–4 — the sibling unit's layer discipline, cited | prohibition]`]`

### §4.4 Its post-release constraint — the ceiling, stated

**`LIVE/INSTALL` PRESUMES REGISTRY STATE — five published names and a published main package — SO IT CANNOT RUN BEFORE THE RELEASE ACT AND ITS FIGURE CANNOT EXIST AT THIS UNIT'S GATES 1–4.** **ITS PHASE IS THEREFORE MARKED BLOCKED IN THE PLAN (`Q10`, blocked by `Q9`), IT IS ADDITIVE AND POST-RELEASE BY CONSTRUCTION, AND RUNNING IT EARLY IS A STOP CONDITION** (`implementation-plan.md §3` and `§5` · stop condition `I`). `[A1 docs/specs/storeroom-publish-proposal-review.md §5.1 item 5 — the ceiling, restated | ordering claim]`

### §4.5 The unit's own node legs beside it, and the primary acceptance layer

1. **THE UNIT'S OWN PRIMARY ACCEPTANCE LAYER IS THE RESOLUTION RULE'S DRIVE** — the load path's state machine, every state answered once — **at `ENVELOPE/PURE`, its SITE the repository root** (`implementation-plan.md §4`).
2. **THE DECLARED LEGS, EACH WITH ITS COMMAND, ITS SITE AND ITS LAYER:** this unit's red set and its node cells run under **`node --test bindings/js/test/*.test.mjs`**, taken at **the repository root**, at **`ENVELOPE/PURE`**; the sibling crate's own Rust leg is **`cargo test --manifest-path bindings/js/Cargo.toml --offline --no-fail-fast`**, taken at **the repository root**, at **`ENVELOPE/PURE`**, **with its manifest named explicitly so the resolution cannot become the root package's** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/package.md §4.2 item 4 — the sibling crate's declared command and its manifest rule | cross-reference]`).
3. **AND NO `APP`-GREEN IS CLAIMED FROM ANY NODE GREEN OR ANY `cargo` LEG**: **a node-suite green is `ENVELOPE/PURE`-green and never `APP`-green** (`[A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/acceptance.md §4.2 item 3 — the rule no pass may break | prohibition]`).
4. **AND THE ONE FIGURE THIS UNIT OWES FROM ITS OWN GATES IS THE RED SET'S OWN REPORTED FAILING SET, PRINTED BEFORE ANY IMPLEMENTATION, WITH ITS SITE AND ITS LAYER** (`implementation-plan.md §2` · `Q2`; `§5.0`). `[A1 | numeric or census claim]`
